    ck26:4-en 
    Could you tell me Sabrina's email
    address from the Marketing
    department?
    		 
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?result
    WHERE
    {
      ?department pv:name "Marketing" .
      ?person pv:memberOf ?department .
      ?person pv:email ?result .
      ?person pv:name ?name .
      FILTER (CONTAINS(?name, "Sabrina"))
    }
    		 
    {
        "Sabrina.Geiger@company.org": 1
    }
    		 
    SELECT DISTINCT ?email WHERE {
      ?emp <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/memberOf> ?dept .
      ?dept <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/name> ?deptName .
      FILTER(CONTAINS(LCASE(?deptName), "marketing"))
      ?emp <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/name> ?name .
      FILTER(CONTAINS(LCASE(?name), "sabrina"))
      ?emp <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/email> ?email .
    }
    		 
    {
        "Sabrina.Geiger@company.org": 1
    }
    		 
    {
        "set_P": 1.0,
        "set_recall": 1.0,
        "set_F": 1.0

    Which encoder is the most

    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?result
    WHERE
    {
      ?result pv:hasCategory <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/prod-cat-Encoder> .
      ?result pv:price ?priceR .
      ?priceR pv:amount ?price .
      ?result pv:hasSupplier ?supplier .
      ?supplier pv:addressCountry ?country .
      FILTER ( (?country = "France") ||  (?country = "Germany") )
    }
    ORDER BY ASC(?price)
    LIMIT 1

        "http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/hw-P453-8155326": 1
    }

      ?supplier <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/addressCountry> ?country .
      FILTER(CONTAINS(LCASE(STR(?country)), "france") || CONTAINS(LCASE(STR(?country)), "germany"))
      ?product <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/price> ?priceObj .

    ck26:25-en 
    Which coil has the greatest
    density?
    		 
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?result
    WHERE
    {
      ?result pv:hasCategory <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/prod-cat-Coil> .
      ?result pv:width_mm ?width_mm .
      ?result pv:depth_mm ?depth_mm .
      ?result pv:height_mm ?height_mm .
      ?result pv:weight_g ?weight_g .
      BIND(?width_mm * ?depth_mm * ?height_mm / 1000000000 AS ?volume_m3)
      BIND(?weight_g / 1000 AS ?weight_kg)
      BIND(?weight_kg / ?volume_m3 AS ?density)
    }
    ORDER BY DESC(?density)
    LIMIT 1
    		 
    {

    SELECT DISTINCT ?product ?label ?density WHERE {
      ?product <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/name> ?label .
      FILTER(CONTAINS(LCASE(STR(?label)), "coil"))
      ?product <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/weight_g> ?weight .
      ?product <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/height_mm> ?height .
      ?product <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/width_mm> ?width .
      ?product <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/depth_mm> ?depth .
      BIND((xsd:decimal(?weight)) / (xsd:decimal(?height) * xsd:decimal(?width) * xsd:decimal(?depth)) AS ?density)
    }
    ORDER BY DESC(?density)
    LIMIT 1

    Welche Spule hat die größte Dichte?
    		 
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?result
    WHERE
    {
      ?result pv:hasCategory <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/prod-cat-Coil> .
      ?result pv:width_mm ?width_mm .
      ?result pv:depth_mm ?depth_mm .
      ?result pv:height_mm ?height_mm .
      ?result pv:weight_g ?weight_g .
      BIND(?width_mm * ?depth_mm * ?height_mm / 1000000000 AS ?volume_m3)
      BIND(?weight_g / 1000 AS ?weight_kg)
      BIND(?weight_kg / ?volume_m3 AS ?density)
    }
    ORDER BY DESC(?density)
    LIMIT 1
    		 
    {
        "http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/hw-N832-1055352": 1
    }
    		 
    SELECT DISTINCT ?product ?label WHERE {
      ?product <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/name> ?label .

      ?product <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/height_mm> ?height .
      ?product <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/width_mm> ?width .
      ?product <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/depth_mm> ?depth .
      BIND((?weight / (?height * ?width * ?depth)) AS ?density)
    }
    ORDER BY DESC(?density)
    LIMIT 1

    ck26:28-en 
    Is there a service that can handle
    a BOM containing parts sourced from
    Russia?
    		 
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    ASK
    WHERE {
      { # all BOMs with parts from russia
        SELECT ?bom
        WHERE {
          ?bom pv:hasBomPart ?bomPart .
          ?bomPart pv:hasPart ?hw .
          ?hw pv:hasSupplier ?suppl .
          ?suppl pv:addressCountry ?country .
          FILTER (CONTAINS(LCASE(?country), LCASE("Russia")))
        }
      }

      # services eligible for parts associated with these BOMs?
      ?s a pv:Service .
      ?s pv:eligibleFor ?hw .
      ?bom pv:hasBomPart ?bomPart .
      ?bomPart pv:hasPart ?hw .
    }
    		 
    {
        "true": 1
    }
    		 
    ASK WHERE {
      ?service <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/eligibleFor> ?product .
      ?bom <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/hasBomPart> ?bomPart .
      ?bomPart <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/hasPart> ?part .
      ?part <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/hasSupplier> ?supplier .
      {
        ?supplier <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/addressCountry> "Russia" .
      } UNION {
        ?supplier <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/country> <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Russia> .
      }
    }
    		 
    {
        "true": 1
    }
    		 
    {
        "set_P": 1.0,
        "set_recall": 1.0,
        "set_F": 1.0
    }

      { # all BOMs with parts from russia
        SELECT ?bom
        WHERE {
          ?bom pv:hasBomPart ?bomPart .
          ?bomPart pv:hasPart ?hw .
          ?hw pv:hasSupplier ?suppl .
          ?suppl pv:addressCountry ?country .
          FILTER (CONTAINS(LCASE(?country), LCASE("Russia")))
        }
      }

      # services eligible for parts associated with these BOMs?
      ?s a pv:Service .
      ?s pv:eligibleFor ?hw .
      ?bom pv:hasBomPart ?bomPart .
      ?bomPart pv:hasPart ?hw .
    }

    Which suppliers—including their
    name, country, and price—provide
    the hardware items that rank from

    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?name ?country ?price
    WHERE {
      ?sup a pv:Supplier ;
        pv:addressCountry ?country ;
      pv:name ?name .
      ?hw a pv:Hardware ;
      pv:price/pv:amount ?price ;
        pv:price/pv:currency "EUR" ;
        pv:hasSupplier ?sup .
    }
    ORDER BY DESC(?price)
    LIMIT 5 OFFSET 10

    WHERE {
      ?dept a pv:Department ;
        pv:name ?name .
      ?emp a pv:Employee ;
        pv:memberOf ?dept .
    }
    GROUP BY ?depty ?name
    HAVING (COUNT(?emp) > 5)

    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?name (COUNT(?emp) AS ?numEmployees)
    WHERE {
      ?dept a pv:Department ;
        pv:name ?name .
      ?emp a pv:Employee ;
        pv:memberOf ?dept .
    }
    GROUP BY ?depty ?name
    HAVING (COUNT(?emp) > 5)

        "Data Services": 1,
        "Product Management": 1,
        "8": 1,
        "9": 1,
        "12": 1
    }
    		 
    SELECT DISTINCT ?deptName (COUNT(?emp) AS ?employeeCount) WHERE {
      ?dept <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/name> ?deptName .
      ?emp <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/Employee> .
      ?emp <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/memberOf> ?dept .
    }
    GROUP BY ?deptName
    HAVING (COUNT(?emp) > 5)
    ORDER BY DESC(?employeeCount)

      ?heaviest <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/hasCategory> ?cat .

    What is the average price in euros
    of each supplier's products,
    rounded to a reasonable amount?
    		 
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?name (ROUND(AVG(?amt) * 100) / 100 AS ?avgEur) WHERE {
      ?sup a pv:Supplier ;
        pv:name ?name .
      ?hw pv:hasSupplier ?sup ;
        pv:price/pv:amount ?amt ;
        pv:price/pv:currency "EUR" .
    }
    GROUP BY ?sup ?name

    SELECT DISTINCT ?supplierName (ROUND(AVG(?amount) * 100) / 100 AS ?avgPriceEUR) WHERE {
      ?product <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/hasSupplier> ?supplier .
      ?supplier <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#label> ?supplierName .
      ?product <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/price> ?priceObj .
      ?priceObj <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/currency> "EUR" .
      ?priceObj <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/amount> ?amount .
    }
    GROUP BY ?supplier ?supplierName
    ORDER BY ?supplierName

    Was ist der durchschnittliche Preis
    in Euro für die Produkte jedes
    Anbieters, gerundet auf einen
    angemessenen Betrag?
    		 
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?name (ROUND(AVG(?amt) * 100) / 100 AS ?avgEur) WHERE {
      ?sup a pv:Supplier ;
        pv:name ?name .
      ?hw pv:hasSupplier ?sup ;
        pv:price/pv:amount ?amt ;
        pv:price/pv:currency "EUR" .
    }
    GROUP BY ?sup ?name

    SELECT DISTINCT ?supplierName (ROUND(AVG(?amount) * 100) / 100 AS ?avgPrice) WHERE {
      ?product <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/hasSupplier> ?supplier .
      ?product <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/price> ?priceObj .
      ?priceObj <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/amount> ?amount .
      ?priceObj <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/currency> ?currency .
      FILTER(CONTAINS(STR(?currency), "EUR"))
      ?supplier <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/name> ?supplierName .
    }
    GROUP BY ?supplier ?supplierName
    ORDER BY ?supplierName

    ck26:33-en 
    Do any departments lack an assigned
    manager?
    		 
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>

    ASK
    WHERE {
      ?dept a pv:Department .
      FILTER NOT EXISTS {
        ?m pv:memberOf ?dept .
        ?m a pv:Manager .
      }
    }
    		 
    {
        "true": 0
    }
    		 
    ASK WHERE {
      ?dept <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/hasManager> ?mgr .
      FILTER NOT EXISTS {
        ?dept <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/hasManager> ?mgr .
      }
    }
    		 
    {
        "true": 0
    }
    		 
    {
        "set_P": 0.0,
        "set_recall": 0.0,
        "set_F": 0.0
    }

      }
    }

    contact list by collecting each

    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?name ?locality ?ccode ?country
    WHERE {
      ?sup a pv:Supplier ;
        pv:name ?name .
      OPTIONAL { ?sup pv:addressLocality ?locality . }
      OPTIONAL { ?sup pv:addressCountryCode ?ccode . }
      OPTIONAL { ?sup pv:addressCountry ?country . }
    }

    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?name ?locality ?ccode ?country
    WHERE {
      ?sup a pv:Supplier ;
        pv:name ?name .
      OPTIONAL { ?sup pv:addressLocality ?locality . }
      OPTIONAL { ?sup pv:addressCountryCode ?ccode . }
      OPTIONAL { ?sup pv:addressCountry ?country . }
    }

    ck26:35-en 
    For each product, could you list
    the other products it's compatible
    with and show the price differences
    between them?
    		 
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv:    <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?prod ?compatible (?prodAmount - ?compatibleAmount AS ?priceDiff)
    WHERE {
      ?prodClass rdfs:subClassOf* pv:Product .
      ?prod a ?prodClass ;
        pv:compatibleProduct ?compatible .
      ?prod pv:price/pv:amount ?prodAmount ;
        pv:price/pv:currency "EUR" .
      ?compatible pv:price/pv:amount ?compatibleAmount ;
        pv:price/pv:currency "EUR" .
    }

    SELECT DISTINCT ?productName ?compatibleProductName ?amount1 ?currency1 ?amount2 ?currency2 ?priceDifference WHERE {
      ?product <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#label> ?productName .
      ?product <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/compatibleProduct> ?compatibleProduct .
      ?compatibleProduct <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#label> ?compatibleProductName .
      ?product <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/price> ?price1 .
      ?price1 <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/amount> ?amount1 .
      ?price1 <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/currency> ?currency1 .
      ?compatibleProduct <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/price> ?price2 .
      ?price2 <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/amount> ?amount2 .
      ?price2 <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/currency> ?currency2 .
      BIND(ABS(?amount1 - ?amount2) AS ?priceDifference)
    }
    ORDER BY ?productName ?compatibleProductName

    ck26:35-de 
    Könnten Sie für jedes Produkt
    auflisten, mit welchen anderen
    Produkten es kompatibel ist, und
    die Preisunterschiede zwischen
    diesen Produkten anzeigen?
    		 
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv:    <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?prod ?compatible (?prodAmount - ?compatibleAmount AS ?priceDiff)
    WHERE {
      ?prodClass rdfs:subClassOf* pv:Product .
      ?prod a ?prodClass ;
        pv:compatibleProduct ?compatible .
      ?prod pv:price/pv:amount ?prodAmount ;
        pv:price/pv:currency "EUR" .
      ?compatible pv:price/pv:amount ?compatibleAmount ;
        pv:price/pv:currency "EUR" .
    }

    SELECT DISTINCT ?productName ?compatibleProductName ?amount1 ?currency1 ?amount2 ?currency2 WHERE {
      ?product <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/compatibleProduct> ?compatibleProduct .
      ?product <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#label> ?productName .
      ?compatibleProduct <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#label> ?compatibleProductName .
      OPTIONAL {
        ?product <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/price> ?price1 .
        ?price1 <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/amount> ?amount1 .
        OPTIONAL {
          ?price1 <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/currency> ?currency1 .
        }
      }
      OPTIONAL {
        ?compatibleProduct <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/price> ?price2 .
        ?price2 <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/amount> ?amount2 .
        OPTIONAL {
          ?price2 <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/currency> ?currency2 .
        }
      }
    }
    ORDER BY ?productName ?compatibleProductName

    ck26:36-en 
    Which product area is most
    relevant, and what are the top
    three associated skills?
    		 
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?cat ?name
    WHERE {
      ?hw a pv:Hardware ;
        pv:hasCategory ?cat .
      ?cat pv:name ?name .
    }
    GROUP BY ?cat ?name
    ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(*))
    LIMIT 3
    		 
    {
        "http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/prod-cat-Compensator": 1,
        "http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/prod-cat-Crystal": 1,
        "http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/prod-cat-LCD": 1,
        "Compensator": 1,
        "Crystal": 1,
        "LCD": 1
    }
    		 
    SELECT DISTINCT ?categoryLabel (COUNT(?emp) AS ?empCount) WHERE {
      ?emp <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/areaOfExpertise> ?category .
      ?category <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/name> ?categoryLabel .
    }
    GROUP BY ?categoryLabel
    ORDER BY DESC(?empCount)
    LIMIT 1
    		 
    {
        "Compensator": 1,
        "11": 1
    }

    For each bill of materials, show
    the count of parts and the total
    material quantity, but display only
    those BOMs with more than 600
    items, sorted in descending order.
    		 
    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?bom (COUNT(?part) AS ?partCount) (SUM(?qty) AS ?totalQty)
    WHERE {
      ?bom a pv:BillOfMaterial ;
        pv:hasBomPart ?part .
      ?part pv:quantity ?qty_ .
      BIND(xsd:int(?qty_) AS ?qty)
    }
    GROUP BY ?bom
    HAVING (SUM(?qty) > 600)
    ORDER BY DESC(SUM(?qty))

    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?name ?email ?phone ?dept ?manager
    WHERE {
      ?agentClass rdfs:subClassOf* pv:Agent .
      ?empl a ?agentClass ;
        pv:name ?name .
      OPTIONAL { ?empl pv:email ?email . }
      OPTIONAL { ?empl pv:phone ?phone . }
      OPTIONAL { ?empl pv:memberOf/pv:name ?dept . }
      OPTIONAL { ?empl pv:hasManager/pv:name ?manager . }
    }

    SELECT DISTINCT ?employeeName ?email ?phone ?departmentName ?managerName WHERE {
      ?emp <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/name> ?employeeName .
      OPTIONAL { ?emp <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/email> ?email . }
      OPTIONAL { ?emp <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/phone> ?phone . }
      OPTIONAL {
        ?emp <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/memberOf> ?dept .
        ?dept <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/name> ?departmentName .
      }
      OPTIONAL {
        ?emp <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/hasManager> ?mgr .
        ?mgr <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/name> ?managerName .
      }
      OPTIONAL {
        ?mgr <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/hasDirectReport> ?emp .
        ?mgr <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/name> ?managerName .
      }
      FILTER(
        EXISTS { ?emp <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/email> ?anyEmail . } ||

    ck26:39-en 
    Can you list the dimensions of
    hardware items that are wider than
    they are tall and have a depth
    under 50 mm?
    		 
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?hw ?width ?height ?depth
    WHERE {
      ?hw a pv:Hardware ;
        pv:width_mm ?width ;
        pv:height_mm ?height ;
        pv:depth_mm ?depth .
      FILTER (?width > ?height && ?depth < 50)
    }

    ck26:39-de 
    Können Sie die Abmessungen von
    Hardware-Artikeln auflisten, die
    breiter als hoch sind und eine
    Tiefe von unter 50 mm haben?
    		 
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?hw ?width ?height ?depth
    WHERE {
      ?hw a pv:Hardware ;
        pv:width_mm ?width ;
        pv:height_mm ?height ;
        pv:depth_mm ?depth .
      FILTER (?width > ?height && ?depth < 50)
    }

    SELECT DISTINCT ?name ?width_mm ?height_mm ?depth_mm WHERE {
      ?hw <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/width_mm> ?width_mm .
      ?hw <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/height_mm> ?height_mm .
      ?hw <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/depth_mm> ?depth_mm .
      ?hw <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/name> ?name .
      FILTER (xsd:float(?width_mm) > xsd:float(?height_mm) && xsd:float(?depth_mm) < 50)
    }

    ck26:40-en 
    Could you list the IDs and names of
    hardware items that don't have an
    active product manager?
    		 
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?hw ?id ?name
    WHERE {
      ?hw a pv:Hardware ;
        pv:id ?id ;
        pv:name ?name .
      FILTER NOT EXISTS { ?hw pv:hasProductManager/pv:name [] . }
    }

    ck26:41-en 
    What share of each manager's
    overall team works in the same
    department as the manager?
    		 
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?m ?name (?deptTeam / ?fullteam * 100 AS ?pct)
    WHERE {

      ?m pv:name ?name .

      { # full team
        SELECT ?m (COUNT(*) AS ?fullteam) WHERE {
          ?e pv:hasManager ?m .
        } GROUP BY ?m
      }

      { # team in same dept
        SELECT ?m (COUNT(*) AS ?deptTeam) WHERE {
          ?m pv:memberOf ?dept .
          ?e pv:hasManager ?m .
          ?e pv:memberOf ?dept .
        } GROUP BY ?m
      }
    }
    		 
    {

        "http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/empl-Waldtraud.Kuttner%40company.org": 1,
        "http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/empl-Thomas.Mueller%40company.org": 1,

    SELECT DISTINCT ?managerName (COUNT(DISTINCT ?report) AS ?totalReports) (COUNT(DISTINCT ?sameDeptReport) AS ?sameDeptReports) (xsd:decimal(COUNT(DISTINCT ?sameDeptReport)) / xsd:decimal(COUNT(DISTINCT ?report)) AS ?share) WHERE {
      ?manager <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#type> <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/Manager> .
      ?manager <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#label> ?managerName .
      ?manager <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/memberOf> ?managerDept .
      ?manager <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/hasDirectReport> ?report .
      OPTIONAL {
        ?report <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/memberOf> ?reportDept .
        FILTER(?reportDept = ?managerDept)
        BIND(?report AS ?sameDeptReport)

    ck26:42-en 
    Which bill of materials has the
    highest average unit cost for its
    hardware components, and what is
    that average?
    		 
    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?bom (ROUND(AVG(?spst / ?sq) * 100) / 100 AS ?avgUnitCost)
    WHERE {
      {
        SELECT ?bom (SUM(?partSubTotal) AS ?spst) (SUM(xsd:int(?quant)) AS ?sq)
        WHERE {
          ?bom a pv:BillOfMaterial ;
            pv:hasBomPart ?part .
          ?part pv:quantity ?quant .
          ?part pv:hasPart/pv:price/pv:amount ?amount .
          ?part pv:hasPart/pv:price/pv:currency "EUR" .
          BIND(xsd:int(?quant) * ?amount AS ?partSubTotal)
        }
        GROUP BY ?bom
      }
    }
    GROUP BY ?bom
    ORDER BY DESC(ROUND(AVG(?spst / ?sq) * 100) / 100)
    LIMIT 1

    ck26:43-de 
    Könnten Sie Beispiele für
    Produktkompatibilitätszyklen nennen
    - Fälle, in denen Produkt A angibt,
    dass es mit Produkt B kompatibel
    ist, und Produkt B wiederum Produkt
    A als kompatibel auflistet?
    		 
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?a ?b
    WHERE {
      ?a pv:compatibleProduct ?b .
      FILTER (STR(?a) < STR(?b))
    }

    ck26:44-en 
    Which hardware items fall within
    the top 10% of widths, and what are
    their IDs, names, and width
    measurements?
    		 
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?id ?name ?width
    WHERE {
      { # get min-max range
        SELECT (MIN(?width) AS ?minW) (MAX(?width) AS ?maxW)
        WHERE {
          ?hw a pv:Hardware ;
            pv:width_mm ?width .
        }
      }

      # get 90. percentile
      BIND(?minW + (0.9 * (?maxW - ?minW)) AS ?pct90)

      ?hw a pv:Hardware ;
        pv:id ?id ;
        pv:name ?name ;
        pv:width_mm ?width .

      FILTER(?width >= ?pct90)
    }
    ORDER BY DESC(?width)

    Welche Hardware-Komponenten gehören
    zu den 10% mit der größten Breite,
    und welche IDs, Namen und
    Breitenmaße haben sie?
    		 
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?id ?name ?width
    WHERE {
      { # get min-max range
        SELECT (MIN(?width) AS ?minW) (MAX(?width) AS ?maxW)
        WHERE {
          ?hw a pv:Hardware ;
            pv:width_mm ?width .
        }
      }

      # get 90. percentile
      BIND(?minW + (0.9 * (?maxW - ?minW)) AS ?pct90)

      ?hw a pv:Hardware ;
        pv:id ?id ;
        pv:name ?name ;
        pv:width_mm ?width .

      FILTER(?width >= ?pct90)
    }
    ORDER BY DESC(?width)

    SELECT DISTINCT ?hw ?id ?name ?width WHERE {
      ?hw <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/id> ?id .
      ?hw <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/name> ?name .
      ?hw <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/width_mm> ?width .
      {
        SELECT (COUNT(?hw2) AS ?total) WHERE {
          ?hw2 <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/width_mm> ?w2 .
        }
      }
      {
        SELECT (MAX(?w3) AS ?maxWidth) (MIN(?w3) AS ?minWidth) WHERE {
          ?hw3 <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/width_mm> ?w3 .
        }
      }
      BIND(CEIL(?total * 0.1) AS ?topN)
      {
        SELECT ?thresholdWidth WHERE {
          {
            SELECT ?w4 WHERE {
              ?hw4 <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/width_mm> ?w4 .
            }
            ORDER BY DESC(?w4)
            LIMIT 1
            OFFSET 0
          }
          BIND(?w4 AS ?thresholdWidth)
        }
      }
      FILTER(?width >= ?thresholdWidth)
    }
    ORDER BY DESC(?width)

    ck26:46-en 
    Which five suppliers have the
    highest average reliability across
    their products?
    		 
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?result (AVG(?reliabilityIndex) AS ?averageReliabilityIndex)
    WHERE
    {
      ?hardware pv:reliabilityIndex ?reliabilityIndex .
      ?hardware pv:hasSupplier ?result .
    }
    GROUP BY ?result
    ORDER BY DESC(?averageReliabilityIndex)

    ck26:50-en 
    Which department is responsible for
    the most products, and how many
    products is that?
    		 
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?result (COUNT(?result) AS ?count)
    WHERE
    {
      ?result pv:responsibleFor ?product .
    }
    GROUP BY ?result
    ORDER BY DESC(?count)
    LIMIT 1
    		 
    {
        "http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/dept-85880": 1,
        "12": 1
    }
    		 
    SELECT DISTINCT ?deptName (COUNT(DISTINCT ?product) AS ?productCount) WHERE {
      ?dept <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/responsibleFor> ?product .
      ?dept <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/name> ?deptName .
    }
    GROUP BY ?dept ?deptName
    ORDER BY DESC(?productCount)
    LIMIT 1

        "12": 1
    }
